# OSV advisories (non-GHSA) — Android 2025 (489 shown; all 489 in `data/osv/Android/2025.jsonl`)

### ASB-A-193031925 — 
- Aliases: A-193031925, CVE-2024-49734 | Published: 2025-01-01 | Modified: 2026-11-16 | Severity: None
- Affected Android/platform/packages/modules/Connectivity: introduced:15-next:0 fixed:15-next:2025-01-01 versions: 15-next
- Affected Android/platform/packages/modules/Connectivity: introduced:15:0 fixed:15:2025-01-01 versions: 15
- Affected Android/platform/frameworks/libs/net: introduced:14:0 fixed:14:2025-01-01 versions: 14
- Affected Android/platform/packages/modules/Connectivity: introduced:14:0 fixed:14:2025-01-01 versions: 14

In multiple functions of ConnectivityService.java, there is a possible way for a Wi-Fi AP to determine what site a device has connected to through a VPN due to side channel information disclosure. This could lead to remote information disclosure with no additional execution privileges needed. User interaction is not needed for exploitation.

References:
- https://source.android.com/security/bulletin/2025-01-01 [ADVISORY]
- https://android.googlesource.com/platform/frameworks/libs/net/+/e72c61380c52a4450970556e5936c5ec03fd66fb [FIX]
- https://android.googlesource.com/platform/frameworks/libs/net/+/b20d67cbfc24a54dfe1dc9854bac61fdf52f7913 [FIX]
- https://android.googlesource.com/platform/packages/modules/Connectivity/+/1a0dd8ccde71c2252132d60e5b897fa2f569cc76 [FIX]
- https://android.googlesource.com/platform/packages/modules/Connectivity/+/4e80ab9ca5b9e7db8185cdde240ab0f01a2c0611 [FIX]
- https://android.googlesource.com/platform/packages/modules/Connectivity/+/de7b97573ac92be4cff6ca71a1837b2fe0dcbbab [FIX]
- https://android.googlesource.com/platform/packages/modules/Connectivity/+/c16addaec604d724cf5296f89e606d558128d0cc [FIX]
- https://android.googlesource.com/platform/packages/modules/Connectivity/+/d5d5e5749316d31a63b9b8b132e273d29364401b [FIX]
- https://android.googlesource.com/platform/packages/modules/Connectivity/+/9eb0da27c4ecff37f7b6d2328b8dee6ba766ad1a [FIX]

### ASB-A-251514170 — 
- Aliases: A-251514170, CVE-2024-34722 | Published: 2025-01-01 | Modified: 2026-11-16 | Severity: None
- Affected Android/platform/packages/modules/Bluetooth: introduced:15-next:0 fixed:15-next:2025-01-01 versions: 15-next
- Affected Android/platform/system/bt: introduced:12:0 fixed:12:2025-01-01 versions: 12
- Affected Android/platform/system/bt: introduced:12L:0 fixed:12L:2025-01-01 versions: 12L
- Affected Android/platform/packages/modules/Bluetooth: introduced:13:0 fixed:13:2025-01-01 versions: 13
- Affected Android/platform/packages/modules/Bluetooth: introduced:14:0 fixed:14:2025-01-01 versions: 14

In smp_proc_rand of smp_act.cc, there is a possible authentication bypass during legacy BLE pairing due to incorrect implementation of a protocol. This could lead to remote escalation of privilege with no additional execution privileges needed. User interaction is not needed for exploitation.

References:
- https://source.android.com/security/bulletin/2025-01-01 [ADVISORY]
- https://android.googlesource.com/platform/packages/modules/Bluetooth/+/317b4e293cefe18fe8c58c1de0c4a6741bd05629 [FIX]
- https://android.googlesource.com/platform/packages/modules/Bluetooth/+/6ad3d749f7f632787f29710ce23736e10d2969bf [FIX]

In onHandleForceStop of VoiceInteractionManagerService.java, there is a bug that could cause the system to incorrectly revert to the default assistant application when a user-selected assistant is forcibly stopped due to a logic error in the code. This could lead to local escalation of privilege where the default assistant app is  automatically granted ROLE_ASSISTANT with no additional execution privileges needed. User interaction is not needed for exploitation.

- https://source.android.com/security/bulletin/2025-05-01 [ADVISORY]

In multiple functions of RoleService.java, there is a possible permission squatting vulnerability due to a logic error in the code. This could lead to local escalation of privilege on versions of Android where android.permission.MANAGE_DEFAULT_APPLICATIONS was not defined with no additional execution privileges needed. User interaction is not needed for exploitation.

The conversation details an out-of-bounds (OOB) write vulnerability in the Broadcom (BCM) WiFi driver. Specifically, the wl_cfg80211_wbtext_table_config function is vulnerable. The vulnerability arises from a heap buffer overflow due to the btcfg->count variable being controlled by firmware, leading to an uncontrolled memcpy.

- Affected Android/:unknown:: introduced:Pixel-family specific:0 fixed:Pixel-family specific:2025-09-05 versions: Pixel-family specific

In draw_surface_image() of abl/android/lib/draw/draw.c, there is a possible out of bounds write due to a heap buffer overflow. This could lead to local escalation of privilege via USB fastboot, after a bootloader unlock, with no additional execution privileges needed. User interaction is needed for exploitation.
